        Idea:
Implement Phase 2 (Three step kinds: code, attested, nested) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per the settled decisions in docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md. Phase 2 scope: introduce the `code`, `attested`, and `nested` step kinds in the schema and runner. `code` is a strict superset of today's `python` and `command` runtime kinds — preserve backward compat. `attested` requires instructions, produces, and ack with identity (--agent or --actor matching ARTAGENTS_ACTOR); self-acks rejected. `nested` is data — the gate sees the child plan's structure for hashing/pinning; forbid code-step-calls-orchestrator. Do NOT implement produces-with-inline-checks in this phase (Phase 3). Do NOT implement repeat:{until/for_each} (Phase 3). Do NOT implement DSL or new lifecycle verbs (Phases 4 and 5). Phase 1 kernel (events.jsonl + plain hash chain + plan hash pin + gate above dispatch + active_run.json) is already committed and shipped — use it. Keep additive: existing JSON manifests with python/command runtime kinds keep working, existing pack mechanism stays. Add focused tests for: code step accepts new schema and old-style python/command manifests; attested step requires identity-pinned ack + evidence; nested step exposes child plan structure to the gate; code-step-calls-orchestrator is rejected at validation time. Single-host, honor-based with logging.

User notes and answers:
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-001 three step kinds: Task mode has exactly `code`, `attested`, and `nested` so execution, evidence, and delegation stay distinct.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-002 repeat body attributes: Iteration and fan-out are `repeat.until` and `repeat.for_each` body attributes so they apply uniformly to steps and nested bodies.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-003 plan naming: Runtime uses immutable `plan.json` instead of checklist naming because the structure is a tree with nested bodies and loops.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-004 event hash chain: `events.jsonl` uses plain `sha256(prev_hash + canonical_event_json)` with no HMAC because V1 needs an edit tripwire, not keyed tamper resistance.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-005 no index database: V1 ships without `.index.db` because file walking and grep over `events.jsonl` are adequate until measured otherwise.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-006 committed DSL: `artagents.orchestrate` Python DSL is the only committed authoring representation so source review happens in Python.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-007 generated build JSON: `<pack>/build/<orch>.json` is gitignored and root `.gitignore` excludes pack build outputs because runtime pins generated artifacts.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-008 per-project CAS: V1 uses only per-project `<slug>/.cas/<sha256>` storage because shared CAS adds coordination without current need.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-009 code boundary: `code` is deterministic argv, a strict superset of `RUNTIME_KINDS={python,command}`, may target `artagents executors run <name>`, and must not target `artagents orchestrators run`.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-010 nested boundary: Sub-orchestrator delegation is exclusively `nested` so the gate can hash and pin child structure.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-011 gate checks: The gate validates active run presence, plan hash, event chain, and incoming command match before dispatch and prints the exact recovery command on rejection.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-012 gate ordering: The gate runs before `_prepare_project_request()` and `thread_wrapper.begin_orchestrator_run()` so rejected commands create zero project-run side effects.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-013 provenance surface: `events.jsonl` is the single write surface for task-mode provenance while thread provenance remains in `runs/<id>/run.json`.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-014 lifecycle dispatch: Task-mode lifecycle verbs extend `artagents/pipeline.py:15` and the existing `artagents orchestrators` CLI remains unchanged.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-015 task kernel namespace: New kernel modules live in `artagents/core/task/` to isolate gate, events, active-run, and env logic from legacy runners.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-016 structure guardrails: `TOP_LEVEL_ARTAGENTS_DIRS` adds `orchestrate` and `verify`, with `tests/test_doctor_setup.py` updated so doctor accepts the new packages.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-017 task run env: `ARTAGENTS_TASK_RUN_ID` is the integration surface for orchestrator, executor, and hype child calls to attach to the parent task run.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-018 child output preservation: Task mode preserves child output dirs and mirrors hype artifacts under `runs/<task-run-id>/steps/<step-id>/produces/` while standalone behavior stays unchanged so `tests/test_project_runs.py` keeps passing.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-019 attestor identity: Agent attestations require `--agent`, human attestations require `--actor` matching `ARTAGENTS_ACTOR`, and self-acks are rejected.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-020 ack rules: `approve` advances, `retry` is only valid after verifier failure, `iterate --feedback` is only valid for `repeat.until=user_approves`, and `abort` ends the run.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-021 inline produces checks: Produces inline checks replace verifier substeps while `passes_test` remains a normal `code` step.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-022 semantic checks: `author check` rejects sentinel-only attested outputs because non-trivial artifacts need semantic validation.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-023 stop-hook preamble: Every `artagents next` output includes the prohibition preamble verbatim because repeated injection fights context decay.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-024 additive migration: Existing `OrchestratorDefinition` JSON manifests and `RUNTIME_KINDS={python,command}` keep working because V1 migration is additive.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-025 honor model: V1 is single-host and honor-based with strong logging because it does not defend against malicious local agents.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-026 cut points: Phase 3 is the frozen-plan runner ship point and Phase 5 is the author-friendly lifecycle ship point.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-027 canonical migration: `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/` is the canonical migration example because it exercises the existing executor pipeline and additive pack layout.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-028 phasing order: V1 implementation follows build phases 1-9 so kernel, step semantics, authoring, lifecycle, nudge, CAS, inbox, and golden tests land in dependency order.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-029 V1 infrastructure limits: V1 excludes daemon, web server, HMAC/keyed crypto, `.index.db`, and shared CAS so the implementation stays file-based, inspectable, and additive.

  {
    "id": "T4",
    "description": "Replace the flat cursor with a path-stack walker and dispatch by kind in `artagents/core/task/gate.py` per Step 4. Replace the existing `cursor = sum(1 for event in events if event.get('kind') == 'step_completed')` (~gate.py:67-69) with `derive_cursor(plan, events) -> CursorPath` that replays `events.jsonl` left-to-right with a frame stack initialized to `[Frame(plan=plan, child_index=0)]`: on `nested_entered` look up the step at the current path, push `Frame(plan=current_step.plan, child_index=0)`; on `nested_exited` pop and increment the parent frame's `child_index`; on `step_completed` or `step_attested` increment the top frame's `child_index`; `step_dispatched` does not advance. Result is a path tuple pointing at the next step or a `done` sentinel when root frame's `child_index == len(steps)`. Add an entry-loop after cursor derivation: while the step at the cursor is a `NestedStep`, compute `child_plan_hash = compute_plan_hash` over the inlined child plan's serialized form, emit `nested_entered`, push the frame, recompute the cursor; when a frame's `child_index == len(steps)`, emit `nested_exited` (returncode 0 on clean fall-through), pop, continue. Implement per-kind command matching in `gate_command`: for `code` keep `command == step.command` strict equality; for `attested` add `match_attested_command(incoming: str, expected_prefix: str) -> tuple[bool, AttestedArgs]` that tokenizes `incoming` via `shlex.split`, splits off recognized identity/evidence flags (`--agent <id>`, `--actor <name>`, `--evidence <path>` repeatable), rejoins remainder via `shlex.quote` and compares to `expected_prefix`, and returns parsed `AttestedArgs(agent: str | None, actor: str | None, evidence: tuple[str,...])`; `nested` is not caller-driven (handled by entry-loop). Implement attested gating rules: reject when neither `--agent` nor `--actor` is present, or both are present; reject when `step.ack.kind == \"agent\"` and only `--actor` provided (or vice versa); when `kind == \"actor\"` require `task_actor_env() == actor_value` and reject as **self-ack** when `run_started.actor == actor_value` and `task_actor_env() == actor_value` (recovery message: `artagents next --project <slug>` for all mismatch classes). On success, emit `step_attested` directly inside `gate_command` (no companion `record_dispatch_complete` for attested \u2014 the event itself advances the cursor). Path discipline: every event the gate writes must carry the path-qualified `plan_step_id` constructed from the current frame stack (e.g., `\"s2/c1\"`); top-level steps use their bare id. Add kernel-level helpers next to `record_dispatch_complete`: `record_step_attested(decision, attestor_kind, attestor_id, evidence)`, `record_nested_entered(decision, child_plan_hash)`, `record_nested_exited(decision, returncode)` \u2014 public for symmetry but the gate's auto-traversal uses private helpers internally; reserved for Phase 5 lifecycle verbs. Add a one-line module comment documenting that Phase 2 attested/nested are kernel-only and Phase 5 will add `artagents ack` / `artagents next` verbs that drive these helpers. Keep this module under ~400 lines.",

    {
      "id": "T4",
      "description": "Replace the flat cursor with a path-stack walker and dispatch by kind in `artagents/core/task/gate.py` per Step 4. Replace the existing `cursor = sum(1 for event in events if event.get('kind') == 'step_completed')` (~gate.py:67-69) with `derive_cursor(plan, events) -> CursorPath` that replays `events.jsonl` left-to-right with a frame stack initialized to `[Frame(plan=plan, child_index=0)]`: on `nested_entered` look up the step at the current path, push `Frame(plan=current_step.plan, child_index=0)`; on `nested_exited` pop and increment the parent frame's `child_index`; on `step_completed` or `step_attested` increment the top frame's `child_index`; `step_dispatched` does not advance. Result is a path tuple pointing at the next step or a `done` sentinel when root frame's `child_index == len(steps)`. Add an entry-loop after cursor derivation: while the step at the cursor is a `NestedStep`, compute `child_plan_hash = compute_plan_hash` over the inlined child plan's serialized form, emit `nested_entered`, push the frame, recompute the cursor; when a frame's `child_index == len(steps)`, emit `nested_exited` (returncode 0 on clean fall-through), pop, continue. Implement per-kind command matching in `gate_command`: for `code` keep `command == step.command` strict equality; for `attested` add `match_attested_command(incoming: str, expected_prefix: str) -> tuple[bool, AttestedArgs]` that tokenizes `incoming` via `shlex.split`, splits off recognized identity/evidence flags (`--agent <id>`, `--actor <name>`, `--evidence <path>` repeatable), rejoins remainder via `shlex.quote` and compares to `expected_prefix`, and returns parsed `AttestedArgs(agent: str | None, actor: str | None, evidence: tuple[str,...])`; `nested` is not caller-driven (handled by entry-loop). Implement attested gating rules: reject when neither `--agent` nor `--actor` is present, or both are present; reject when `step.ack.kind == \"agent\"` and only `--actor` provided (or vice versa); when `kind == \"actor\"` require `task_actor_env() == actor_value` and reject as **self-ack** when `run_started.actor == actor_value` and `task_actor_env() == actor_value` (recovery message: `artagents next --project <slug>` for all mismatch classes). On success, emit `step_attested` directly inside `gate_command` (no companion `record_dispatch_complete` for attested \u2014 the event itself advances the cursor). Path discipline: every event the gate writes must carry the path-qualified `plan_step_id` constructed from the current frame stack (e.g., `\"s2/c1\"`); top-level steps use their bare id. Add kernel-level helpers next to `record_dispatch_complete`: `record_step_attested(decision, attestor_kind, attestor_id, evidence)`, `record_nested_entered(decision, child_plan_hash)`, `record_nested_exited(decision, returncode)` \u2014 public for symmetry but the gate's auto-traversal uses private helpers internally; reserved for Phase 5 lifecycle verbs. Add a one-line module comment documenting that Phase 2 attested/nested are kernel-only and Phase 5 will add `artagents ack` / `artagents next` verbs that drive these helpers. Keep this module under ~400 lines.",

      "id": "T6",
      "description": "Write the new and extended kernel tests per Step 7. CREATE `tests/test_task_plan_schema.py` covering: (a) legacy `{id, command}` plan loads as a single `code`-kind step (back-compat); (b) explicit `kind: \"code\"` form normalizes such that the *normalized serialized form* (`to_dict` output) is identical to the normalized form of the legacy literal \u2014 assert this rather than asserting `compute_plan_hash` equality between raw legacy and raw canonical literals (per FLAG-008: `compute_plan_hash` hashes the on-disk canonical-event-json of the raw payload, so legacy and canonical raw literals will hash differently \u2014 what we assert is `to_dict` round-trip determinism on the canonical form); (c) `kind: \"code\"` with command `python3 -m artagents orchestrators run ...` is rejected at `load_plan` with `TaskPlanError` mentioning \"nested\"; (d) sibling-id collision in one frame is rejected; identical leaf ids across different subtrees are accepted; (e) pack-side `OrchestratorDefinition` legacy manifests still validate via `validate_orchestrator_definition` with one fixture per `RUNTIME_KINDS={python,command}`. CREATE `tests/test_task_kernel_attested.py` driving the gate directly with synthetic argv (no CLI verb yet): reject when neither `--agent` nor `--actor` present (recovery `artagents next ...`); reject when both present; `step.ack.kind=\"agent\"` succeeds with `--agent <id>`, emits `step_attested` with `attestor_kind=\"agent\"`, `attestor_id=<id>`, `evidence=()` and a path-qualified `plan_step_id`; `step.ack.kind=\"actor\"` requires `ARTAGENTS_ACTOR=<same>`, mismatch rejects; **self-ack rejected** when `run_started.actor == ARTAGENTS_ACTOR == --actor` for the same attested step (reason \"self-ack rejected\"); evidence `--evidence a.json --evidence b.json` records both, canonical command match still succeeds because the matcher strips evidence tokens; `verify_chain` returns `ok=True` after attested events. CREATE `tests/test_task_kernel_nested.py` driving the gate to walk a nested plan: mutating any child step changes parent `compute_plan_hash` (structural pinning); walking parent `[s1, s2(nested=[c1, c2]), s3]` auto-emits `nested_entered(\"s2\", child_plan_hash)`, accepts `c1` (event `step_completed` with `plan_step_id=\"s2/c1\"`), then `c2` (`\"s2/c2\"`), auto-emits `nested_exited(\"s2\", returncode=0)`, then accepts `s3`; two-level nesting `[s1, s2(nested=[c1, c2(nested=[g1])])]` produces path sequence `s1, s2/c1, s2/c2/g1` with matching enter/exit events; sibling subtrees with reused leaf ids `[s1(nested=[c1]), s2(nested=[c1])]` disambiguate via `s1/c1` vs `s2/c1`; `derive_cursor` is correct on partial replay (resume mid-nested-walk). EXTEND `tests/test_task_kernel_gate.py` with: regression that existing Phase 1 helper (`_write_plan` style with bare `{id, command}`) still gates correctly, and that Phase 1 events for top-level code steps keep their bare `plan_step_id` payload shape.",

  "meta_commentary": "Phase 2 is purely additive kernel work in `artagents/core/task/` \u2014 no CLI verbs, no DSL, no new top-level package dirs. The load-bearing change is replacing the flat-counter cursor with a path-stack walker (T4); everything else either feeds it (T1 schema, T2 events) or verifies it (T6 tests). Implementation order: T1 \u2192 (T2, T3 in parallel are fine but T2 depends on T1) \u2192 T4 \u2192 T5 audit \u2192 T6 tests \u2192 T7 validation. Four flags from the critique are still open (FLAG-005 through FLAG-008) \u2014 they are minor and addressed in the watch_items rather than blocking the plan. The single most likely place to slip is FLAG-008: the schema test must assert `to_dict` normalized-form equality, NOT raw-hash equality between legacy and canonical literals \u2014 `compute_plan_hash` hashes the on-disk canonical-event-json of the raw payload, so two literals that normalize to the same `to_dict` output will still produce different hashes when hashed directly. Second-most-likely slip is FLAG-007: ensure `step_attested` is emitted inline in `gate_command` and the public `record_step_attested` helper has zero callers in Phase 2 (it's reserved for Phase 5). Verify with a grep that nothing in `artagents/core/task/gate.py` calls `record_step_attested`. Third pitfall: `ARTAGENTS_ACTOR` must NOT be in `child_subprocess_env` \u2014 operator identity is per-process by design (SD-019). The plan is otherwise tightly scoped \u2014 every step has a specific file and line-range anchor in the existing kernel. There are no human-only setup actions for this phase: no env vars to set (ARTAGENTS_ACTOR is set per-test by the test harness), no infra, no migrations, no deploys. All work is repo-local code edits and `pytest` runs.",

81	@dataclass
82	class CursorPath:
83	    frames: list[_Frame] = field(default_factory=list)
84	
85	    @property
86	    def at_root_done(self) -> bool:
87	        return len(self.frames) == 1 and self.frames[-1].child_index >= len(self.frames[-1].plan.steps)
88	
89	    @property
90	    def top_exhausted(self) -> bool:
91	        top = self.frames[-1]
92	        return top.child_index >= len(top.plan.steps)

94	
95	def derive_cursor(plan: TaskPlan, events: Sequence[dict[str, Any]]) -> CursorPath:
96	    """Replay ``events.jsonl`` left-to-right to reconstruct the path-stack cursor.
97	
98	    Reconstructible from events alone (supports partial-replay resume):
99	    ``nested_entered`` pushes, ``nested_exited`` pops + advances parent,
100	    ``step_completed`` / ``step_attested`` advance the top frame,
101	    ``step_dispatched`` and ``run_started`` do not advance.
102	    """
103	    frames: list[_Frame] = [_Frame(plan=plan, path_prefix=(), child_index=0)]
104	    for event in events:
105	        kind = event.get("kind")
106	        if kind == "nested_entered":
107	            top = frames[-1]
108	            if top.child_index >= len(top.plan.steps):
109	                raise TaskRunGateError(
110	                    reason="nested_entered points past end of frame",
111	                    recovery="inspect events.jsonl",
112	                )
113	            step = top.plan.steps[top.child_index]
114	            if not isinstance(step, NestedStep):
115	                raise TaskRunGateError(
116	                    reason="nested_entered did not land on a NestedStep",
117	                    recovery="inspect events.jsonl",
118	                )
119	            frames.append(
120	                _Frame(
121	                    plan=step.plan,
122	                    path_prefix=top.path_prefix + (step.id,),
123	                    child_index=0,
124	                )
125	            )
126	        elif kind == "nested_exited":
127	            if len(frames) <= 1:
128	                raise TaskRunGateError(
129	                    reason="nested_exited at root frame",
130	                    recovery="inspect events.jsonl",
131	                )
132	            frames.pop()
133	            frames[-1].child_index += 1
134	        elif kind in ("step_completed", "step_attested"):
135	            frames[-1].child_index += 1
136	        # step_dispatched / run_started: no-op
137	    return CursorPath(frames=frames)
138	
139	
140	def gate_command(
141	    slug: str,
142	    command: str,
143	    argv: Sequence[str],
144	    *,
145	    root: str | Path | None = None,
146	    reentry: bool = False,
147	) -> GateDecision:
148	    active_run = read_active_run(slug, root=root)
149	    if active_run is None:
150	        if not is_in_task_run(slug):
151	            return GateDecision(active=False)
152	        _reject(slug, "active_run.json is missing", abort=True)
153	
154	    project_root = project_dir(slug, root=root)
155	    plan_path = project_root / "plan.json"
156	    plan_hash = compute_plan_hash(plan_path)
157	    if plan_hash != active_run["plan_hash"]:
158	        _reject(slug, "plan.json hash does not match active_run.json pin", abort=True)
159	
160	    run_id = active_run["run_id"]
161	    events_path = project_root / "runs" / run_id / "events.jsonl"
162	    ok, _last_index, error = verify_chain(events_path)
163	    if not ok:
164	        _reject(slug, error or "events.jsonl chain integrity check failed", abort=True)
165	
166	    plan = load_plan(plan_path)
167	    events = read_events(events_path)
168	    cursor = derive_cursor(plan, events)
169	    run_started_actor = _find_run_started_actor(events)
170	
171	    # Auto-traverse nested steps: emit nested_entered/exited until we land on a leaf
172	    # (CodeStep or AttestedStep) or determine the plan is exhausted.
173	    while True:
174	        if cursor.at_root_done:
175	            _reject(slug, "plan is exhausted", abort=True)
176	        if cursor.top_exhausted:
177	            top = cursor.frames[-1]
178	            exit_path_str = STEP_PATH_SEP.join(top.path_prefix)
179	            append_event(events_path, make_nested_exited_event(exit_path_str, 0))
180	            cursor.frames.pop()
181	            cursor.frames[-1].child_index += 1
182	            continue
183	        top = cursor.frames[-1]
184	        current_step = top.plan.steps[top.child_index]
185	        current_path = top.path_prefix + (current_step.id,)
186	        path_str = STEP_PATH_SEP.join(current_path)
187	        if isinstance(current_step, NestedStep):
188	            child_hash = _compute_inline_plan_hash(current_step.plan)
189	            append_event(events_path, make_nested_entered_event(path_str, child_hash))
190	            cursor.frames.append(
191	                _Frame(plan=current_step.plan, path_prefix=current_path, child_index=0)
192	            )
193	            continue
194	        break
195	
196	    if isinstance(current_step, CodeStep):
197	        return _dispatch_code(
198	            slug=slug,
199	            command=command,
200	            step=current_step,
201	            path_str=path_str,
202	            events_path=events_path,
203	            run_id=run_id,
204	            reentry=reentry,
205	        )
206	    if isinstance(current_step, AttestedStep):
207	        return _dispatch_attested(
208	            slug=slug,
209	            command=command,
210	            step=current_step,
211	            path_str=path_str,
212	            events_path=events_path,
213	            run_id=run_id,
214	            run_started_actor=run_started_actor,
215	        )
216	    raise TaskRunGateError(
217	        reason=f"unexpected step kind: {type(current_step).__name__}",
218	        recovery=f"artagents next --project {slug}",
219	    )
220

222	def _dispatch_code(
223	    *,
224	    slug: str,
225	    command: str,
226	    step: CodeStep,
227	    path_str: str,
228	    events_path: Path,
229	    run_id: str,
230	    reentry: bool,
231	) -> GateDecision:
232	    if command != step.command:
233	        _reject(slug, "incoming command does not match plan[cursor]", abort=False)
234	
235	    if reentry:
236	        events = read_events(events_path)
237	        latest = events[-1] if events else None
238	        if (
239	            not isinstance(latest, dict)
240	            or latest.get("kind") != "step_dispatched"
241	            or latest.get("plan_step_id") != path_str
242	            or latest.get("command") != command
243	        ):
244	            _reject(slug, "incoming command does not match plan[cursor]", abort=False)
245	        apply_task_run_env(run_id, slug, path_str)
246	        return GateDecision(
247	            active=True,
248	            run_id=run_id,
249	            plan_step_id=path_str,
250	            events_path=events_path,
251	            reentry=True,
252	            step_kind="code",
253	        )
254	
255	    append_event(events_path, make_step_dispatched_event(path_str, command))
256	    apply_task_run_env(run_id, slug, path_str)
257	    return GateDecision(
258	        active=True,
259	        run_id=run_id,

288	    append_event(

290	        make_step_attested_event(path_str, attestor_kind, attestor_id, args.evidence),
291	    )
292	    return GateDecision(
293	        active=True,
294	        run_id=run_id,

296	        events_path=events_path,
297	        reentry=False,

301	
302	def match_attested_command(incoming: str, expected_prefix: str) -> tuple[bool, AttestedArgs]:
303	    """Strip ``--agent``/``--actor``/``--evidence`` (repeatable) tokens from
304	    ``incoming`` and compare the canonical remainder to ``expected_prefix``.
305	    """
306	    try:
307	        tokens = shlex.split(incoming)
308	    except ValueError:
309	        return False, AttestedArgs(agent=None, actor=None, evidence=())
310	    agent: str | None = None
311	    actor: str | None = None
312	    evidence: list[str] = []
313	    remaining: list[str] = []
314	    i = 0
315	    while i < len(tokens):
316	        token = tokens[i]
317	        if token == "--agent" and i + 1 < len(tokens):
318	            agent = tokens[i + 1]
319	            i += 2
320	            continue
321	        if token == "--actor" and i + 1 < len(tokens):
322	            actor = tokens[i + 1]
323	            i += 2
324	            continue
325	        if token == "--evidence" and i + 1 < len(tokens):
326	            evidence.append(tokens[i + 1])
327	            i += 2
328	            continue
329	        remaining.append(token)
330	        i += 1
331	    rejoined = " ".join(shlex.quote(token) for token in remaining)
332	    matched = rejoined == expected_prefix
333	    return matched, AttestedArgs(agent=agent, actor=actor, evidence=tuple(evidence))
334	
335	
336	def _validate_attested_identity(
337	    *,
338	    slug: str,
339	    step: AttestedStep,
340	    args: AttestedArgs,
341	    run_started_actor: str | None,
342	) -> tuple[str, str]:
343	    if args.agent is None and args.actor is None:
344	        _reject(slug, "attested step requires --agent or --actor", abort=False)
345	    if args.agent is not None and args.actor is not None:
346	        _reject(slug, "attested step rejects both --agent and --actor", abort=False)
347	    if step.ack.kind == "agent":
348	        if args.agent is None:
349	            _reject(slug, "attested step ack.kind=agent requires --agent", abort=False)
350	        return "agent", args.agent  # type: ignore[return-value]
351	    # ack.kind == "actor"
352	    if args.actor is None:
353	        _reject(slug, "attested step ack.kind=actor requires --actor", abort=False)
354	    if task_actor_env() != args.actor:
355	        _reject(slug, "attested --actor does not match ARTAGENTS_ACTOR env", abort=False)
356	    # FLAG-005: self-ack rejection only applies to actor attestations because agents
357	    # do not start runs in V1; an agent_id on run_started would be required to
358	    # symmetrically block agent self-acks, which is out of scope for Phase 2.
359	    if (
360	        run_started_actor is not None
361	        and run_started_actor == args.actor
362	        and task_actor_env() == args.actor
363	    ):
364	        _reject(slug, "self-ack rejected", abort=False)
365	    return "actor", args.actor  # type: ignore[return-value]
366

368	def record_dispatch_complete(decision: GateDecision, returncode: int) -> None:
369	    if not decision.active or decision.events_path is None or decision.plan_step_id is None:
370	        return
371	    if decision.step_kind == "attested":
372	        # attested steps are advanced by step_attested itself; do not double-emit
373	        return
374	    append_event(
375	        decision.events_path,
376	        make_step_completed_event(decision.plan_step_id, returncode),
377	    )

399	    append_event(
400	        decision.events_path,
401	        make_nested_entered_event(decision.plan_step_id, child_plan_hash),
402	    )
403	
404	
405	def record_nested_exited(decision: GateDecision, returncode: int) -> None:
406	    """Reserved for Phase 5 lifecycle verbs; gate emits inline in Phase 2."""
407	    if not decision.active or decision.events_path is None or decision.plan_step_id is None:
408	        return
409	    append_event(
410	        decision.events_path,
411	        make_nested_exited_event(decision.plan_step_id, returncode),
412	    )
413	
414	
415	def command_for_argv(argv: Sequence[str]) -> str:
416	    tokens = [str(token) for token in argv]
417	    if len(tokens) >= 3 and Path(tokens[0]).name.startswith("python") and tokens[1:3] == ["-m", "artagents"]:
418	        tokens = tokens[3:]
419	    elif tokens and Path(tokens[0]).name.endswith("artagents"):
420	        tokens = tokens[1:]
421	    return " ".join(shlex.quote(token) for token in tokens)
422	
423	
424	def _compute_inline_plan_hash(plan: TaskPlan) -> str:
425	    digest = hashlib.sha256(canonical_event_json(plan.to_dict()).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
426	    return f"sha256:{digest}"
427	
428	
429	def _find_run_started_actor(events: Sequence[dict[str, Any]]) -> str | None:
430	    for event in events:
431	        if event.get("kind") == "run_started":
432	            actor = event.get("actor")
433	            return actor if isinstance(actor, str) else None
434	    return None
435	
436	
437	def _reject(slug: str, reason: str, *, abort: bool) -> None:
438	    verb = "abort" if abort else "next"
439	    raise TaskRunGateError(reason=reason, recovery=f"artagents {verb} --project {slug}")
440
```

 class TaskRunGateError(RuntimeError):
@@ -36,6 +61,80 @@ class GateDecision:
     plan_step_id: str | None = None
     events_path: Path | None = None
     reentry: bool = False
+    step_kind: str | None = None
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class AttestedArgs:
+    agent: str | None
+    actor: str | None
+    evidence: tuple[str, ...]
+
+
+@dataclass
+class _Frame:
+    plan: TaskPlan
+    path_prefix: tuple[str, ...]
+    child_index: int = 0
+
+
+@dataclass
+class CursorPath:
+    frames: list[_Frame] = field(default_factory=list)
+
+    @property
+    def at_root_done(self) -> bool:
+        return len(self.frames) == 1 and self.frames[-1].child_index >= len(self.frames[-1].plan.steps)
+
+    @property
+    def top_exhausted(self) -> bool:
+        top = self.frames[-1]
+        return top.child_index >= len(top.plan.steps)
+
+
+def derive_cursor(plan: TaskPlan, events: Sequence[dict[str, Any]]) -> CursorPath:
+    """Replay ``events.jsonl`` left-to-right to reconstruct the path-stack cursor.
+
+    Reconstructible from events alone (supports partial-replay resume):
+    ``nested_entered`` pushes, ``nested_exited`` pops + advances parent,
+    ``step_completed`` / ``step_attested`` advance the top frame,
+    ``step_dispatched`` and ``run_started`` do not advance.

User notes and answers:
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-001 three step kinds: Task mode has exactly `code`, `attested`, and `nested` so execution, evidence, and delegation stay distinct.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-002 repeat body attributes: Iteration and fan-out are `repeat.until` and `repeat.for_each` body attributes so they apply uniformly to steps and nested bodies.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-003 plan naming: Runtime uses immutable `plan.json` instead of checklist naming because the structure is a tree with nested bodies and loops.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-004 event hash chain: `events.jsonl` uses plain `sha256(prev_hash + canonical_event_json)` with no HMAC because V1 needs an edit tripwire, not keyed tamper resistance.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-005 no index database: V1 ships without `.index.db` because file walking and grep over `events.jsonl` are adequate until measured otherwise.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-006 committed DSL: `artagents.orchestrate` Python DSL is the only committed authoring representation so source review happens in Python.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-007 generated build JSON: `<pack>/build/<orch>.json` is gitignored and root `.gitignore` excludes pack build outputs because runtime pins generated artifacts.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-008 per-project CAS: V1 uses only per-project `<slug>/.cas/<sha256>` storage because shared CAS adds coordination without current need.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-009 code boundary: `code` is deterministic argv, a strict superset of `RUNTIME_KINDS={python,command}`, may target `artagents executors run <name>`, and must not target `artagents orchestrators run`.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-010 nested boundary: Sub-orchestrator delegation is exclusively `nested` so the gate can hash and pin child structure.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-011 gate checks: The gate validates active run presence, plan hash, event chain, and incoming command match before dispatch and prints the exact recovery command on rejection.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-012 gate ordering: The gate runs before `_prepare_project_request()` and `thread_wrapper.begin_orchestrator_run()` so rejected commands create zero project-run side effects.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-013 provenance surface: `events.jsonl` is the single write surface for task-mode provenance while thread provenance remains in `runs/<id>/run.json`.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-014 lifecycle dispatch: Task-mode lifecycle verbs extend `artagents/pipeline.py:15` and the existing `artagents orchestrators` CLI remains unchanged.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-015 task kernel namespace: New kernel modules live in `artagents/core/task/` to isolate gate, events, active-run, and env logic from legacy runners.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-016 structure guardrails: `TOP_LEVEL_ARTAGENTS_DIRS` adds `orchestrate` and `verify`, with `tests/test_doctor_setup.py` updated so doctor accepts the new packages.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-017 task run env: `ARTAGENTS_TASK_RUN_ID` is the integration surface for orchestrator, executor, and hype child calls to attach to the parent task run.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-018 child output preservation: Task mode preserves child output dirs and mirrors hype artifacts under `runs/<task-run-id>/steps/<step-id>/produces/` while standalone behavior stays unchanged so `tests/test_project_runs.py` keeps passing.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-019 attestor identity: Agent attestations require `--agent`, human attestations require `--actor` matching `ARTAGENTS_ACTOR`, and self-acks are rejected.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-020 ack rules: `approve` advances, `retry` is only valid after verifier failure, `iterate --feedback` is only valid for `repeat.until=user_approves`, and `abort` ends the run.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-021 inline produces checks: Produces inline checks replace verifier substeps while `passes_test` remains a normal `code` step.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-022 semantic checks: `author check` rejects sentinel-only attested outputs because non-trivial artifacts need semantic validation.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-023 stop-hook preamble: Every `artagents next` output includes the prohibition preamble verbatim because repeated injection fights context decay.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-024 additive migration: Existing `OrchestratorDefinition` JSON manifests and `RUNTIME_KINDS={python,command}` keep working because V1 migration is additive.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-025 honor model: V1 is single-host and honor-based with strong logging because it does not defend against malicious local agents.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-026 cut points: Phase 3 is the frozen-plan runner ship point and Phase 5 is the author-friendly lifecycle ship point.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-027 canonical migration: `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/` is the canonical migration example because it exercises the existing executor pipeline and additive pack layout.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-028 phasing order: V1 implementation follows build phases 1-9 so kernel, step semantics, authoring, lifecycle, nudge, CAS, inbox, and golden tests land in dependency order.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-029 V1 infrastructure limits: V1 excludes daemon, web server, HMAC/keyed crypto, `.index.db`, and shared CAS so the implementation stays file-based, inspectable, and additive.

      "id": "T4",
      "description": "Replace the flat cursor with a path-stack walker and dispatch by kind in `artagents/core/task/gate.py` per Step 4. Replace the existing `cursor = sum(1 for event in events if event.get('kind') == 'step_completed')` (~gate.py:67-69) with `derive_cursor(plan, events) -> CursorPath` that replays `events.jsonl` left-to-right with a frame stack initialized to `[Frame(plan=plan, child_index=0)]`: on `nested_entered` look up the step at the current path, push `Frame(plan=current_step.plan, child_index=0)`; on `nested_exited` pop and increment the parent frame's `child_index`; on `step_completed` or `step_attested` increment the top frame's `child_index`; `step_dispatched` does not advance. Result is a path tuple pointing at the next step or a `done` sentinel when root frame's `child_index == len(steps)`. Add an entry-loop after cursor derivation: while the step at the cursor is a `NestedStep`, compute `child_plan_hash = compute_plan_hash` over the inlined child plan's serialized form, emit `nested_entered`, push the frame, recompute the cursor; when a frame's `child_index == len(steps)`, emit `nested_exited` (returncode 0 on clean fall-through), pop, continue. Implement per-kind command matching in `gate_command`: for `code` keep `command == step.command` strict equality; for `attested` add `match_attested_command(incoming: str, expected_prefix: str) -> tuple[bool, AttestedArgs]` that tokenizes `incoming` via `shlex.split`, splits off recognized identity/evidence flags (`--agent <id>`, `--actor <name>`, `--evidence <path>` repeatable), rejoins remainder via `shlex.quote` and compares to `expected_prefix`, and returns parsed `AttestedArgs(agent: str | None, actor: str | None, evidence: tuple[str,...])`; `nested` is not caller-driven (handled by entry-loop). Implement attested gating rules: reject when neither `--agent` nor `--actor` is present, or both are present; reject when `step.ack.kind == \"agent\"` and only `--actor` provided (or vice versa); when `kind == \"actor\"` require `task_actor_env() == actor_value` and reject as **self-ack** when `run_started.actor == actor_value` and `task_actor_env() == actor_value` (recovery message: `artagents next --project <slug>` for all mismatch classes). On success, emit `step_attested` directly inside `gate_command` (no companion `record_dispatch_complete` for attested \u2014 the event itself advances the cursor). Path discipline: every event the gate writes must carry the path-qualified `plan_step_id` constructed from the current frame stack (e.g., `\"s2/c1\"`); top-level steps use their bare id. Add kernel-level helpers next to `record_dispatch_complete`: `record_step_attested(decision, attestor_kind, attestor_id, evidence)`, `record_nested_entered(decision, child_plan_hash)`, `record_nested_exited(decision, returncode)` \u2014 public for symmetry but the gate's auto-traversal uses private helpers internally; reserved for Phase 5 lifecycle verbs. 20	class TaskPlanError(ValueError):
21	    """Raised when plan.json is malformed."""
22	
23	
24	@dataclass(frozen=True)
25	class AckRule:
26	    kind: Literal["agent", "actor"]
27	
28	
29	@dataclass(frozen=True)

31	    id: str

36	@dataclass(frozen=True)

56	@dataclass(frozen=True)
57	class TaskPlan:

62	    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
63	        return {
64	            "plan_id": self.plan_id,
65	            "version": self.version,
66	            "steps": [_step_to_dict(step) for step in self.steps],

70	def load_plan(plan_path: str | Path) -> TaskPlan:

75	def compute_plan_hash(plan_path: str | Path) -> str:

78	    digest = hashlib.sha256(canonical_event_json(payload).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()

95	def iter_steps_with_path(plan: TaskPlan) -> Iterator[tuple[tuple[str, ...], TaskPlanStep]]:
96	    """Yield ``(path, step)`` in pre-order for every step in the plan tree."""
97	
98	    def _walk(steps: tuple[TaskPlanStep, ...], prefix: tuple[str, ...]) -> Iterator[tuple[tuple[str, ...], TaskPlanStep]]:
99	        for step in steps:
100	            path = prefix + (step.id,)
101	            yield path, step
102	            if isinstance(step, NestedStep):
103	                yield from _walk(step.plan.steps, path)
104	
105	    yield from _walk(plan.steps, ())

107	
108	def _step_to_dict(step: TaskPlanStep) -> dict[str, Any]:
109	    if isinstance(step, CodeStep):
110	        return {"id": step.id, "kind": "code", "command": step.command}
111	    if isinstance(step, AttestedStep):
112	        out: dict[str, Any] = {
113	            "id": step.id,
114	            "kind": "attested",
115	            "command": step.command,
116	            "instructions": step.instructions,
117	            "ack": {"kind": step.ack.kind},
118	        }
119	        if step.produces:
120	            out["produces"] = list(step.produces)
121	        return out
122	    if isinstance(step, NestedStep):
123	        return {
124	            "id": step.id,
125	            "kind": "nested",
126	            "plan": step.plan.to_dict(),
127	        }
128	    raise TaskPlanError(f"unknown step type: {type(step)!r}")
129	
130	
131	def _read_plan_payload(plan_path: str | Path) -> Any:
132	    path = Path(plan_path)
133	    try:
134	        return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
135	    except FileNotFoundError:
136	        raise
137	    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
138	        raise TaskPlanError(f"invalid JSON in {path}: {exc.msg}") from exc
139	    except OSError as exc:
140	        raise TaskPlanError(f"failed to read {path}: {exc}") from exc
141	
142	
143	def _validate_plan(payload: Any, *, _is_root: bool = True) -> TaskPlan:
144	    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
145	        raise TaskPlanError("plan must be an object")
146	    plan_id = payload.get("plan_id")
147	    version = payload.get("version")
148	    steps = payload.get("steps")
149	    if not isinstance(plan_id, str) or not plan_id:
150	        raise TaskPlanError("plan plan_id must be a non-empty string")
151	    if version != 1 or isinstance(version, bool):
152	        raise TaskPlanError("plan version must be 1")
153	    if not isinstance(steps, list):
154	        raise TaskPlanError("plan steps must be a list")
155	
156	    validated_steps: list[TaskPlanStep] = []
157	    for index, step in enumerate(steps):
158	        validated_steps.append(_validate_step(step, index))
159	
160	    plan = TaskPlan(plan_id=plan_id, version=1, steps=tuple(validated_steps))
161	    if _is_root:
162	        _assert_unique_paths(plan)
163	    return plan
164	
165	
166	def _validate_step(step: Any, index: int) -> TaskPlanStep:
167	    if not isinstance(step, dict):
168	        raise TaskPlanError(f"plan steps[{index}] must be an object")
169	    kind = step.get("kind", "code")
170	    if kind == "code":
171	        return _validate_code_step(step, index)
172	    if kind == "attested":
173	        return _validate_attested_step(step, index)
174	    if kind == "nested":
175	        return _validate_nested_step(step, index)
176	    raise TaskPlanError(
177	        f"plan steps[{index}].kind must be one of {TASK_STEP_KINDS}, got {kind!r}"
178	    )
179	
180	
181	def _validate_code_step(step: dict[str, Any], index: int) -> CodeStep:
182	    step_id = step.get("id")
183	    command = step.get("command")
184	    if not isinstance(step_id, str) or not step_id:
185	        raise TaskPlanError(f"plan steps[{index}].id must be a non-empty string")
186	    if not isinstance(command, str) or not command:
187	        raise TaskPlanError(f"plan steps[{index}].command must be a non-empty string")
188	    _reject_orchestrators_run(command, index)
189	    return CodeStep(id=validate_run_id(step_id), command=command)
190	
191	
192	def _validate_attested_step(step: dict[str, Any], index: int) -> AttestedStep:
193	    step_id = step.get("id")
194	    command = step.get("command")
195	    instructions = step.get("instructions")
196	    produces = step.get("produces", [])
197	    ack = step.get("ack")
198	    if not isinstance(step_id, str) or not step_id:
199	        raise TaskPlanError(f"plan steps[{index}].id must be a non-empty string")
200	    if not isinstance(command, str) or not command:
201	        raise TaskPlanError(f"plan steps[{index}].command must be a non-empty string")
202	    if not isinstance(instructions, str) or not instructions:
203	        raise TaskPlanError(
204	            f"plan steps[{index}].instructions must be a non-empty string for attested step"
205	        )
206	    if not isinstance(produces, list) or not all(isinstance(p, str) and p for p in produces):
207	        raise TaskPlanError(
208	            f"plan steps[{index}].produces must be a list of non-empty strings"
209	        )
210	    if not isinstance(ack, dict):
211	        raise TaskPlanError(f"plan steps[{index}].ack must be an object")
212	    ack_kind = ack.get("kind")
213	    if ack_kind not in {"agent", "actor"}:
214	        raise TaskPlanError(
215	            f"plan steps[{index}].ack.kind must be 'agent' or 'actor', got {ack_kind!r}"
216	        )
217	    return AttestedStep(
218	        id=validate_run_id(step_id),
219	        command=command,
220	        instructions=instructions,
221	        produces=tuple(produces),
222	        ack=AckRule(kind=ack_kind),
223	    )
224	
225	
226	def _validate_nested_step(step: dict[str, Any], index: int) -> NestedStep:
227	    step_id = step.get("id")
228	    child_plan = step.get("plan")
229	    if not isinstance(step_id, str) or not step_id:
230	        raise TaskPlanError(f"plan steps[{index}].id must be a non-empty string")
231	    if not isinstance(child_plan, dict):
232	        raise TaskPlanError(
233	            f"plan steps[{index}].plan must be a nested plan object"
234	        )
235	    nested = _validate_plan(child_plan, _is_root=False)
236	    return NestedStep(id=validate_run_id(step_id), plan=nested)
237	
238	
239	def _reject_orchestrators_run(command: str, index: int) -> None:
240	    try:
241	        tokens = shlex.split(command)
242	    except ValueError as exc:
243	        raise TaskPlanError(
244	            f"plan steps[{index}].command is not shell-parseable: {exc}"
245	        ) from exc
246	    tokens = _strip_artagents_prefix(tokens)
247	    if len(tokens) >= 2 and tokens[0] == "orchestrators" and tokens[1] == "run":
248	        raise TaskPlanError(
249	            "code step argv targets 'artagents orchestrators run'; use a nested step"
250	        )
251	
252	
253	def _strip_artagents_prefix(tokens: list[str]) -> list[str]:
254	    if (
255	        len(tokens) >= 3
256	        and Path(tokens[0]).name.startswith("python")
257	        and tokens[1:3] == ["-m", "artagents"]
258	    ):
259	        return tokens[3:]
260	    if tokens and Path(tokens[0]).name.endswith("artagents"):
261	        return tokens[1:]
262	    return tokens
263	
264	
265	def _assert_unique_paths(plan: TaskPlan) -> None:
266	    """Reject sibling-id collisions inside any one frame.
267	
268	    Identical leaf ids in different subtrees are accepted because their full paths differ.
269	    """
270	
271	    def _check(steps: tuple[TaskPlanStep, ...], prefix: tuple[str, ...]) -> None:
272	        seen: set[str] = set()
273	        for step in steps:
274	            if step.id in seen:
275	                location = STEP_PATH_SEP.join(prefix + (step.id,))
276	                raise TaskPlanError(
277	                    f"duplicate step id {step.id!r} among siblings at {location!r}"
278	                )
279	            seen.add(step.id)
280	            if isinstance(step, NestedStep):
281	                _check(step.plan.steps, prefix + (step.id,))
282	
283	    _check(plan.steps, ())

1	"""Hash-chained task-run event log."""
2	
3	from __future__ import annotations
4	
5	import errno
6	import hashlib
7	import json
8	import os
9	from datetime import UTC, datetime
10	from pathlib import Path
11	from typing import Any
12	
13	ZERO_HASH = "sha256:" + "0" * 64
14	
15	
16	class EventLogError(RuntimeError):
17	    """Raised when a task event log cannot be read or written."""
18	
19	
20	def canonical_event_json(event: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
21	    payload = {key: value for key, value in event.items() if key != "hash"}
22	    return json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), ensure_ascii=False)
23	
24	
25	def append_event(path: str | Path, event: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
26	    events_path = Path(path)
27	    ok, _last_index, error = verify_chain(events_path)
28	    if not ok:
29	        raise EventLogError(error or f"invalid event hash chain in {events_path}")
30	
31	    previous_events = read_events(events_path)
32	    prev_hash = previous_events[-1]["hash"] if previous_events else ZERO_HASH
33	
34	    stored = dict(event)
35	    stored.pop("hash", None)
36	    stored["hash"] = _event_hash(prev_hash, stored)
37	
38	    events_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
39	    created = not events_path.exists()
40	    try:
41	        with events_path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
42	            handle.write(json.dumps(stored, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), ensure_ascii=False))
43	            handle.write("\n")
44	            handle.flush()
45	            os.fsync(handle.fileno())
46	        if created:
47	            _fsync_dir(events_path.parent)
48	    except OSError as exc:
49	        raise EventLogError(f"failed to append event to {events_path}: {exc}") from exc
50	    return stored
51	
52	
53	def verify_chain(path: str | Path) -> tuple[bool, int, str | None]:
54	    events_path = Path(path)
55	    try:
56	        handle = events_path.open("r", encoding="utf-8")
57	    except FileNotFoundError:
58	        return True, -1, None
59	    except OSError as exc:
60	        return False, -1, f"failed to read {events_path}: {exc}"
61	
62	    prev_hash = ZERO_HASH
63	    last_index = -1
64	    with handle:
65	        for index, line in enumerate(handle):
66	            if not line.endswith("\n"):
67	                return False, index, f"event log line {index + 1} is not newline-terminated"
68	            raw = line[:-1]
69	            if not raw:
70	                return False, index, f"event log line {index + 1} is empty"
71	            try:
72	                event = json.loads(raw)
73	            except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
74	                return False, index, f"invalid JSON on event log line {index + 1}: {exc.msg}"
75	            if not isinstance(event, dict):
76	                return False, index, f"event log line {index + 1} is not an object"
77	            stored_hash = event.get("hash")
78	            if not isinstance(stored_hash, str):
79	                return False, index, f"event log line {index + 1} is missing hash"
80	            expected_hash = _event_hash(prev_hash, event)
81	            if stored_hash != expected_hash:
82	                return (
83	                    False,
84	                    index,
85	                    f"event log line {index + 1} hash mismatch: expected {expected_hash}, got {stored_hash}",
86	                )
87	            prev_hash = stored_hash
88	            last_index = index
89	    return True, last_index, None
90	
91	
92	def read_events(path: str | Path) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
93	    events_path = Path(path)
94	    try:
95	        with events_path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
96	            return [json.loads(line) for line in handle]
97	    except FileNotFoundError:
98	        return []
99	    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
100	        raise EventLogError(f"invalid JSON in {events_path}: {exc.msg}") from exc
101	    except OSError as exc:
102	        raise EventLogError(f"failed to read {events_path}: {exc}") from exc
103

111	    payload: dict[str, Any] = {

166	    return {

171	    }
172	
173	
174	def _event_hash(prev_hash: str, event: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
175	    digest = hashlib.sha256((prev_hash + canonical_event_json(event)).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
176	    return f"sha256:{digest}"
177	
178	
179	def _utc_now_iso() -> str:
180	    return datetime.now(UTC).isoformat().replace("+00:00", "Z")
181	
182	
183	def _fsync_dir(path: Path) -> None:
184	    flags = getattr(os, "O_DIRECTORY", 0) | os.O_RDONLY
185	    fd: int | None = None
186	    try:
187	        fd = os.open(path, flags)
188	        os.fsync(fd)
189	    except OSError as exc:
190	        if exc.errno not in {errno.EINVAL, errno.ENOTSUP, errno.EBADF}:
191	            raise
192	    finally:
193	        if fd is not None:
194	            os.close(fd)
195

132	def _canonical_child_list(data: dict[str, Any], *, legacy_key: str, canonical_key: str, path: str) -> list[str]:
133	    legacy_present = legacy_key in data

135	    legacy_values = _optional_string_list(data, legacy_key, f"orchestrator.{legacy_key}") if legacy_present else []
136	    canonical_values = _optional_string_list(data, canonical_key, f"orchestrator.{canonical_key}") if canonical_present else []

138	        raise OrchestratorValidationError(
139	            f"orchestrator.{legacy_key} and orchestrator.{canonical_key} conflict; use identical values or only one field"

188	    if raw is None:

192	    data = _require_mapping(raw, path)

200	        env[key] = value

203	        cwd=_optional_nullable_string(data, "cwd", f"{path}.cwd"),

228	def _validate_orchestrator(orchestrator: OrchestratorDefinition) -> None:
229	    _validate_qualified_identifier(orchestrator.id, "orchestrator.id")
230	    _validate_non_empty_string(orchestrator.name, "orchestrator.name")
231	    if orchestrator.kind not in ORCHESTRATOR_KINDS:
232	        raise OrchestratorValidationError(f"orchestrator.kind must be one of {sorted(ORCHESTRATOR_KINDS)}")
233	    _validate_non_empty_string(orchestrator.version, "orchestrator.version")
234	    _validate_runtime(orchestrator.runtime)
235	
236	    input_names = _validate_unique_named(orchestrator.inputs, "input")
237	    output_names = _validate_unique_named(orchestrator.outputs, "output")
238	    placeholders = set(input_names) | set(output_names)
239	    placeholders.update({"out", "brief", "python_exec", "orchestrator_args", "verbose"})
240	
241	    for port in orchestrator.inputs:
242	        _validate_port(port)
243	        if port.placeholder:
244	            _validate_non_empty_identifier(port.placeholder, f"input {port.name!r}.placeholder")
245	            placeholders.add(port.placeholder)
246	    for output in orchestrator.outputs:
247	        _validate_output(output)
248	        if output.placeholder:
249	            _validate_non_empty_identifier(output.placeholder, f"output {output.name!r}.placeholder")
250	            placeholders.add(output.placeholder)
251	        if output.path_template:
252	            _validate_placeholders(output.path_template, placeholders, f"output {output.name!r}.path_template")
253	    for index, child_executor in enumerate(orchestrator.child_executors):
254	        _validate_qualified_identifier(child_executor, f"orchestrator.child_executors[{index}]")
255	    for index, child_orchestrator in enumerate(orchestrator.child_orchestrators):
256	        _validate_qualified_identifier(child_orchestrator, f"orchestrator.child_orchestrators[{index}]")
257	    _validate_cache(orchestrator.cache)
258	    _validate_isolation(orchestrator.isolation)
259	    if orchestrator.runtime.command is not None:
260	        _validate_command(orchestrator.runtime.command, placeholders)
261	
262	
263	def _validate_runtime(runtime: RuntimeSpec) -> None:
264	    if runtime.kind not in RUNTIME_KINDS:
265	        raise OrchestratorValidationError(f"runtime.kind must be one of {sorted(RUNTIME_KINDS)}")
266	    if runtime.kind == "python":
267	        _validate_non_empty_string(runtime.module, "runtime.module")
268	        _validate_non_empty_string(runtime.function, "runtime.function")
269	        if runtime.command is not None:
270	            raise OrchestratorValidationError("python runtime cannot include runtime.command")
271	    if runtime.kind == "command":
272	        if runtime.command is None:
273	            raise OrchestratorValidationError("command runtime requires runtime.command")
274	        if runtime.module is not None or runtime.function is not None:
275	            raise OrchestratorValidationError("command runtime cannot include runtime.module or runtime.function")
276	
277	
278	def _validate_port(port: Port) -> None:
279	    _validate_non_empty_identifier(port.name, "input.name")
280	    if port.required and port.default is not None:
281	        raise OrchestratorValidationError(f"input {port.name!r} cannot be both required and have a default")
282	
283	
284	def _validate_output(output: Output) -> None:
285	    _validate_non_empty_identifier(output.name, "output.name")
286	    if output.mode not in OUTPUT_MODES:
287	        raise OrchestratorValidationError(f"output {output.name!r}.mode must be one of ['create', 'create_or_replace', 'mutate']")
288	
289	
290	def _validate_cache(cache: CachePolicy) -> None:
291	    if cache.mode not in CACHE_MODES:
292	        raise OrchestratorValidationError("cache.mode must be one of ['always_run', 'none', 'sentinel']")
293	    if cache.always_run and cache.sentinels:
294	        raise OrchestratorValidationError("cache.always_run cannot be combined with cache.sentinels")
295	    if cache.mode == "none" and (cache.sentinels or cache.always_run or cache.per_brief):
296	        raise OrchestratorValidationError("cache.mode 'none' cannot include sentinels, always_run, or per_brief")
297	    if cache.mode == "always_run" and not cache.always_run:
298	        raise OrchestratorValidationError("cache.mode 'always_run' requires cache.always_run=true")
299	
300	
301	def _validate_isolation(isolation: IsolationMetadata) -> None:
302	    if isolation.mode not in ISOLATION_MODES:
303	        raise OrchestratorValidationError("isolation.mode must be one of ['in_process', 'subprocess']")
304	
305	
306	def _validate_command(command: CommandSpec, placeholders: set[str]) -> None:
307	    if not command.argv:
308	        raise OrchestratorValidationError("runtime.command.argv must contain at least one argument")
309	    for index, part in enumerate(command.argv):
310	        _validate_non_empty_string(part, f"runtime.command.argv[{index}]")
311	        _validate_placeholders(part, placeholders, f"runtime.command.argv[{index}]")
312	    if command.cwd:
313	        _validate_placeholders(command.cwd, placeholders, "runtime.command.cwd")
314	    for key, value in command.env.items():
315	        _validate_non_empty_string(key, "runtime.command.env key")
316	        _validate_placeholders(value, placeholders, f"runtime.command.env[{key!r}]")
317	
318	
319	def _validate_placeholders(value: str, allowed: set[str], path: str) -> None:
320	    for placeholder in re.findall(r"\{([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)\}", value):
321	        if placeholder not in allowed:
322	            raise OrchestratorValidationError(f"{path} uses unknown placeholder {{{placeholder}}}")
323	
324	
325	def _validate_unique_named(values: tuple[Port, ...] | tuple[Output, ...], label: str) -> set[str]:
326	    names: set[str] = set()
327	    for value in values:
328	        if value.name in names:
329	            raise OrchestratorValidationError(f"duplicate {label} name {value.name!r}")
330	        names.add(value.name)
331	    return names
332	
333	
334	def _validate_non_empty_identifier(value: Any, path: str) -> None:
335	    _validate_non_empty_string(value, path)
336	    if not re.match(r"^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_.-]*$", value):
337	        raise OrchestratorValidationError(f"{path} must start with a letter and contain only letters, numbers, '.', '_' or '-'")
338	
339	
340	def _validate_qualified_identifier(value: Any, path: str) -> None:
341	    _validate_non_empty_identifier(value, path)
342	    if "." not in value or any(not part for part in value.split(".")):
343	        raise OrchestratorValidationError(f"{path} must be qualified as <pack>.<name>")
344	
345	
346	def _validate_non_empty_string(value: Any, path: str) -> None:
347	    if not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip():
348	        raise OrchestratorValidationError(f"{path} must be a non-empty string")
349	
350	
351	def _require_mapping(raw: Any, path: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
352	    if not isinstance(raw, dict):
353	        raise OrchestratorValidationError(f"{path} must be an object")
354	    return raw
355	
356	
357	def _require_string(data: dict[str, Any], key: str, path: str) -> str:
358	    if key not in data:
359	        raise OrchestratorValidationError(f"missing required field {path}")
360	    value = data[key]
361	    _validate_non_empty_string(value, path)
362	    return value
363	
364	
365	def _optional_string(data: dict[str, Any], key: str, path: str, *, default: str = "") -> str:
366	    if key not in data:
367	        return default
368	    value = data[key]
369	    _validate_non_empty_string(value, path)
370	    return value
371	
372	
373	def _optional_nullable_string(data: dict[str, Any], key: str, path: str) -> str | None:
374	    if key not in data or data[key] is None:
375	        return None
376	    value = data[key]
377	    _validate_non_empty_string(value, path)
378	    return value
379	
380	
381	def _optional_bool(data: dict[str, Any], key: str, path: str, *, default: bool) -> bool:
382	    if key not in data:
383	        return default
384	    value = data[key]
385	    if not isinstance(value, bool):
386	        raise OrchestratorValidationError(f"{path} must be a boolean")
387	    return value
388	
389	
390	def _optional_list(data: dict[str, Any], key: str, path: str) -> list[Any]:
391	    if key not in data:
392	        return []
393	    value = data[key]
394	    if not isinstance(value, list):
395	        raise OrchestratorValidationError(f"{path} must be a list")
396	    return value
397	
398	
399	def _string_list(raw: Any, path: str) -> list[str]:
400	    if not isinstance(raw, (list, tuple)):
401	        raise OrchestratorValidationError(f"{path} must be a list")
402	    result: list[str] = []
403	    for index, value in enumerate(raw):
404	        if not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip():
405	            raise OrchestratorValidationError(f"{path}[{index}] must be a non-empty string")
406	        result.append(value)
407	    return result
408	
409	
410	def _optional_string_list(data: dict[str, Any], key: str, path: str) -> list[str]:
411	    if key not in data:
412	        return []
413	    return _string_list(data[key], path)
414	
415	
416	def _drop_none(value: Any) -> Any:
417	    if isinstance(value, dict):
418	        return {key: _drop_none(item) for key, item in value.items() if item is not None}
419	    if isinstance(value, tuple):
420	        return [_drop_none(item) for item in value]
421	    if isinstance(value, list):
422	        return [_drop_none(item) for item in value]
423	    return value
424

1	"""Task-run environment helpers."""
2	
3	from __future__ import annotations
4	
5	import os
6	from collections.abc import Mapping
7	
8	TASK_RUN_ID_ENV = "ARTAGENTS_TASK_RUN_ID"
9	TASK_PROJECT_ENV = "ARTAGENTS_TASK_PROJECT"
10	TASK_STEP_ID_ENV = "ARTAGENTS_TASK_STEP_ID"
11	ARTAGENTS_ACTOR = "ARTAGENTS_ACTOR"
12	
13	
14	def task_project_env() -> str | None:
15	    return os.environ.get(TASK_PROJECT_ENV)
16	
17	
18	def task_run_id_env() -> str | None:
19	    return os.environ.get(TASK_RUN_ID_ENV)
20	
21	
22	def task_step_id_env() -> str | None:
23	    return os.environ.get(TASK_STEP_ID_ENV)
24	
25	
26	def task_actor_env() -> str | None:
27	    return os.environ.get(ARTAGENTS_ACTOR)
28	
29	
30	def is_in_task_run(slug: str | None = None) -> bool:
31	    run_id = task_run_id_env()
32	    if not run_id:
33	        return False
34	    return slug is None or task_project_env() == slug
35	
36	
37	def apply_task_run_env(run_id: str, project_slug: str, step_id: str) -> None:
38	    os.environ[TASK_RUN_ID_ENV] = run_id
39	    os.environ[TASK_PROJECT_ENV] = project_slug
40	    os.environ[TASK_STEP_ID_ENV] = step_id
41	
42	
43	def child_subprocess_env(*, base: Mapping[str, str] | None = None) -> dict[str, str]:
44	    env = dict(os.environ if base is None else base)
45	    env.pop(ARTAGENTS_ACTOR, None)
46	    for key in (TASK_RUN_ID_ENV, TASK_PROJECT_ENV, TASK_STEP_ID_ENV):
47	        value = os.environ.get(key)
48	        if value is not None:
49	            env[key] = value
50	    return env
51

1	from __future__ import annotations

7	from artagents.core.executor import cli as executors_cli

13	def _capture(fn, argv: list[str]) -> tuple[int, str, str]:
14	    stdout = io.StringIO()
15	    stderr = io.StringIO()
16	    with contextlib.redirect_stdout(stdout), contextlib.redirect_stderr(stderr):
17	        result = fn(argv)
18	    return result, stdout.getvalue(), stderr.getvalue()
19	
20	
21	class QualifiedIdEnforcementTest(unittest.TestCase):
22	    def test_executor_schema_rejects_bare_ids(self) -> None:
23	        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ExecutorValidationError, "executor.id must be qualified"):
24	            validate_executor_definition(
25	                {
26	                    "id": "cut",
27	                    "name": "Cut",
28	                    "kind": "built_in",
29	                    "version": "1.0",
30	                }
31	            )
32	
33	    def test_executor_schema_rejects_bare_dependencies(self) -> None:
34	        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ExecutorValidationError, "graph.depends_on"):
35	            validate_executor_definition(
36	                {
37	                    "id": "builtin.cut",
38	                    "name": "Cut",
39	                    "kind": "built_in",
40	                    "version": "1.0",
41	                    "graph": {"depends_on": ["transcribe"]},
42	                }
43	            )
44	
45	    def test_orchestrator_schema_rejects_bare_ids_and_children(self) -> None:
46	        with self.assertRaisesRegex(OrchestratorValidationError, "orchestrator.id must be qualified"):
47	            validate_orchestrator_definition(
48	                {
49	                    "id": "hype",
50	                    "name": "Hype",
51	                    "kind": "built_in",
52	                    "version": "1.0",
53	                    "runtime": {"kind": "command", "command": {"argv": ["echo", "ok"]}},
54	                }
55	            )
56	
57	        with self.assertRaisesRegex(OrchestratorValidationError, "orchestrator.child_executors"):
58	            validate_orchestrator_definition(
59	                {
60	                    "id": "builtin.hype",
61	                    "name": "Hype",
62	                    "kind": "built_in",
63	                    "version": "1.0",
64	                    "runtime": {"kind": "command", "command": {"argv": ["echo", "ok"]}},
65	                    "child_executors": ["cut"],
66	                }
67	            )

69	    def test_cli_rejects_bare_executor_lookup_and_accepts_qualified_id(self) -> None:
70	        result, _, stderr = _capture(executors_cli.main, ["inspect", "cut"])
71	        self.assertEqual(result, 2)
72	        self.assertIn("executor id must be qualified", stderr)
73	
74	        result, stdout, stderr = _capture(executors_cli.main, ["inspect", "builtin.cut"])

1	"""Active task-run pointer helpers."""
2	
3	from __future__ import annotations
4	
5	import re
6	from pathlib import Path
7	from typing import Any
8	
9	from artagents.core.project.jsonio import read_json, write_json_atomic
10	from artagents.core.project.paths import project_dir, validate_run_id
11	
12	_PLAN_HASH_RE = re.compile(r"^sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}$")
13	
14	
15	class ActiveRunError(ValueError):
16	    """Raised when active_run.json is malformed."""
17	
18	
19	def read_active_run(slug: str, *, root: str | Path | None = None) -> dict[str, str] | None:
20	    path = _active_run_path(slug, root=root)
21	    try:
22	        payload = read_json(path)
23	    except FileNotFoundError:
24	        return None
25	    return _validate_active_run(payload)
26	
27	
28	def write_active_run(
29	    slug: str,
30	    *,
31	    run_id: str,
32	    plan_hash: str,
33	    root: str | Path | None = None,
34	) -> dict[str, str]:
35	    payload = _validate_active_run({"run_id": run_id, "plan_hash": plan_hash})
36	    write_json_atomic(_active_run_path(slug, root=root), payload)
37	    return payload
38	
39	
40	def clear_active_run(slug: str, *, root: str | Path | None = None) -> None:
41	    _active_run_path(slug, root=root).unlink(missing_ok=True)
42	
43	
44	def _active_run_path(slug: str, *, root: str | Path | None = None) -> Path:
45	    return project_dir(slug, root=root) / "active_run.json"
46	
47	
48	def _validate_active_run(payload: Any) -> dict[str, str]:
49	    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
50	        raise ActiveRunError("active_run.json must be an object")
51	    run_id = payload.get("run_id")
52	    plan_hash = payload.get("plan_hash")
53	    if not isinstance(run_id, str):
54	        raise ActiveRunError("active_run.json run_id must be a string")
55	    if not isinstance(plan_hash, str) or _PLAN_HASH_RE.fullmatch(plan_hash) is None:
56	        raise ActiveRunError("active_run.json plan_hash must be sha256:<64 lowercase hex>")
57	    return {"run_id": validate_run_id(run_id), "plan_hash": plan_hash}
58

!= mutated_hash\n\n\ndef test_derive_cursor_partial_replay_resumes_mid_nested_walk(\n    tmp_projects_root: Path,\n) -> None:\n    plan_path = _write_nested_plan(tmp_projects_root, _parent_with_two_children())\n    plan = load_plan(plan_path)\n\n    # Walk part-way: complete s1, enter s2, complete c1.\n    d1 = gate_command(\"demo\", \"echo s1\", [], root=tmp_projects_root)\n    record_dispatch_complete(d1, 0)\n    d2 = gate_command(\"demo\", \"echo c1\", [], root=tmp_projects_root)\n    record_dispatch_complete(d2, 0)\n\n    events_path = tmp_projects_root / \"demo\" / \"runs\" / \"run-1\" / \"events.jsonl\"\n    events = read_events(events_path)\n    cursor = derive_cursor(plan, events)\n\n    # Frame stack should be at parent[s2] -> child frame with child_index=1 (c2 next).\n    assert len(cursor.frames) == 2\n    assert cursor.frames[0].child_index == 1  # parent advanced past s1\n    assert cursor.frames[1].child_index == 1  # child advanced past c1\n\n    # Continuation works correctly post-resume.\n    d3 = gate_command(\"demo\", \"echo c2\", [], root=tmp_projects_root)\n    assert d3.plan_step_id == \"s2/c2\"\n\n\ndef test_nested_entered_event_carries_child_plan_hash(tmp_projects_root: Path) -> None:\n    _write_nested_plan(tmp_projects_root, _parent_with_two_children())\n    events_path = tmp_projects_root / \"demo\" / \"runs\" / \"run-1\" / \"events.jsonl\"\n\n    # First gate dispatches s1 -- no nested entry yet.\n    d1 = gate_command(\"demo\", \"echo s1\", [], root=tmp_projects_root)\n    record_dispatch_complete(d1, 0)\n    # Second gate auto-emits nested_entered for s2 before dispatching c1.\n    gate_command(\"demo\", \"echo c1\", [], root=tmp_projects_root)\n\n    events = read_events(events_path)\n    entered = next(e for e in events if e[\"kind\"] == \"nested_entered\")\n    assert entered[\"plan_step_id\"] == \"s2\"\n    assert entered[\"child_plan_hash\"].startswith(\"sha256:\")\n    assert len(entered[\"child_plan_hash\"]) == len(\"sha256:\") + 64\n\n\ndef test_plan_exhausted_at_root_after_nested_walk_rejects(tmp_projects_root: Path) -> None:\n    _write_nested_plan(tmp_projects_root, _parent_with_two_children())\n\n    for command, expected in [\n        (\"echo s1\", \"s1\"),\n        (\"echo c1\", \"s2/c1\"),\n        (\"echo c2\", \"s2/c2\"),\n        (\"echo s3\", \"s3\"),\n    ]:\n        d = gate_command(\"demo\", command, [], root=tmp_projects_root)\n        assert d.plan_step_id == expected\n        record_dispatch_complete(d, 0)\n\n    with pytest.raises(TaskRunGateError) as exc:\n        gate_command(\"demo\", \"echo anything\", [], root=tmp_projects_root)\n    assert exc.value.recovery == \"artagents abort --project demo\"\n"
}
