        Idea:
Implement Phase 2 (Three step kinds: code, attested, nested) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per the settled decisions in docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md. Phase 2 scope: introduce the `code`, `attested`, and `nested` step kinds in the schema and runner. `code` is a strict superset of today's `python` and `command` runtime kinds — preserve backward compat. `attested` requires instructions, produces, and ack with identity (--agent or --actor matching ARTAGENTS_ACTOR); self-acks rejected. `nested` is data — the gate sees the child plan's structure for hashing/pinning; forbid code-step-calls-orchestrator. Do NOT implement produces-with-inline-checks in this phase (Phase 3). Do NOT implement repeat:{until/for_each} (Phase 3). Do NOT implement DSL or new lifecycle verbs (Phases 4 and 5). Phase 1 kernel (events.jsonl + plain hash chain + plan hash pin + gate above dispatch + active_run.json) is already committed and shipped — use it. Keep additive: existing JSON manifests with python/command runtime kinds keep working, existing pack mechanism stays. Add focused tests for: code step accepts new schema and old-style python/command manifests; attested step requires identity-pinned ack + evidence; nested step exposes child plan structure to the gate; code-step-calls-orchestrator is rejected at validation time. Single-host, honor-based with logging.

User notes and answers:
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-001 three step kinds: Task mode has exactly `code`, `attested`, and `nested` so execution, evidence, and delegation stay distinct.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-002 repeat body attributes: Iteration and fan-out are `repeat.until` and `repeat.for_each` body attributes so they apply uniformly to steps and nested bodies.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-003 plan naming: Runtime uses immutable `plan.json` instead of checklist naming because the structure is a tree with nested bodies and loops.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-004 event hash chain: `events.jsonl` uses plain `sha256(prev_hash + canonical_event_json)` with no HMAC because V1 needs an edit tripwire, not keyed tamper resistance.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-005 no index database: V1 ships without `.index.db` because file walking and grep over `events.jsonl` are adequate until measured otherwise.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-006 committed DSL: `artagents.orchestrate` Python DSL is the only committed authoring representation so source review happens in Python.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-007 generated build JSON: `<pack>/build/<orch>.json` is gitignored and root `.gitignore` excludes pack build outputs because runtime pins generated artifacts.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-008 per-project CAS: V1 uses only per-project `<slug>/.cas/<sha256>` storage because shared CAS adds coordination without current need.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-009 code boundary: `code` is deterministic argv, a strict superset of `RUNTIME_KINDS={python,command}`, may target `artagents executors run <name>`, and must not target `artagents orchestrators run`.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-010 nested boundary: Sub-orchestrator delegation is exclusively `nested` so the gate can hash and pin child structure.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-011 gate checks: The gate validates active run presence, plan hash, event chain, and incoming command match before dispatch and prints the exact recovery command on rejection.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-012 gate ordering: The gate runs before `_prepare_project_request()` and `thread_wrapper.begin_orchestrator_run()` so rejected commands create zero project-run side effects.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-013 provenance surface: `events.jsonl` is the single write surface for task-mode provenance while thread provenance remains in `runs/<id>/run.json`.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-014 lifecycle dispatch: Task-mode lifecycle verbs extend `artagents/pipeline.py:15` and the existing `artagents orchestrators` CLI remains unchanged.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-015 task kernel namespace: New kernel modules live in `artagents/core/task/` to isolate gate, events, active-run, and env logic from legacy runners.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-016 structure guardrails: `TOP_LEVEL_ARTAGENTS_DIRS` adds `orchestrate` and `verify`, with `tests/test_doctor_setup.py` updated so doctor accepts the new packages.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-017 task run env: `ARTAGENTS_TASK_RUN_ID` is the integration surface for orchestrator, executor, and hype child calls to attach to the parent task run.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-018 child output preservation: Task mode preserves child output dirs and mirrors hype artifacts under `runs/<task-run-id>/steps/<step-id>/produces/` while standalone behavior stays unchanged so `tests/test_project_runs.py` keeps passing.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-019 attestor identity: Agent attestations require `--agent`, human attestations require `--actor` matching `ARTAGENTS_ACTOR`, and self-acks are rejected.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-020 ack rules: `approve` advances, `retry` is only valid after verifier failure, `iterate --feedback` is only valid for `repeat.until=user_approves`, and `abort` ends the run.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-021 inline produces checks: Produces inline checks replace verifier substeps while `passes_test` remains a normal `code` step.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-022 semantic checks: `author check` rejects sentinel-only attested outputs because non-trivial artifacts need semantic validation.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-023 stop-hook preamble: Every `artagents next` output includes the prohibition preamble verbatim because repeated injection fights context decay.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-024 additive migration: Existing `OrchestratorDefinition` JSON manifests and `RUNTIME_KINDS={python,command}` keep working because V1 migration is additive.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-025 honor model: V1 is single-host and honor-based with strong logging because it does not defend against malicious local agents.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-026 cut points: Phase 3 is the frozen-plan runner ship point and Phase 5 is the author-friendly lifecycle ship point.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-027 canonical migration: `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/` is the canonical migration example because it exercises the existing executor pipeline and additive pack layout.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-028 phasing order: V1 implementation follows build phases 1-9 so kernel, step semantics, authoring, lifecycle, nudge, CAS, inbox, and golden tests land in dependency order.
- Ignored malformed settled decision line: SD-029 V1 infrastructure limits: V1 excludes daemon, web server, HMAC/keyed crypto, `.index.db`, and shared CAS so the implementation stays file-based, inspectable, and additive.

    "description": "Replace the flat cursor with a path-stack walker and dispatch by kind in `artagents/core/task/gate.py` per Step 4. Replace the existing `cursor = sum(1 for event in events if event.get('kind') == 'step_completed')` (~gate.py:67-69) with `derive_cursor(plan, events) -> CursorPath` that replays `events.jsonl` left-to-right with a frame stack initialized to `[Frame(plan=plan, child_index=0)]`: on `nested_entered` look up the step at the current path, push `Frame(plan=current_step.plan, child_index=0)`; on `nested_exited` pop and increment the parent frame's `child_index`; on `step_completed` or `step_attested` increment the top frame's `child_index`; `step_dispatched` does not advance. Result is a path tuple pointing at the next step or a `done` sentinel when root frame's `child_index == len(steps)`. Add an entry-loop after cursor derivation: while the step at the cursor is a `NestedStep`, compute `child_plan_hash = compute_plan_hash` over the inlined child plan's serialized form, emit `nested_entered`, push the frame, recompute the cursor; when a frame's `child_index == len(steps)`, emit `nested_exited` (returncode 0 on clean fall-through), pop, continue. Implement per-kind command matching in `gate_command`: for `code` keep `command == step.command` strict equality; for `attested` add `match_attested_command(incoming: str, expected_prefix: str) -> tuple[bool, AttestedArgs]` that tokenizes `incoming` via `shlex.split`, splits off recognized identity/evidence flags (`--agent <id>`, `--actor <name>`, `--evidence <path>` repeatable), rejoins remainder via `shlex.quote` and compares to `expected_prefix`, and returns parsed `AttestedArgs(agent: str | None, actor: str | None, evidence: tuple[str,...])`; `nested` is not caller-driven (handled by entry-loop). Implement attested gating rules: reject when neither `--agent` nor `--actor` is present, or both are present; reject when `step.ack.kind == \"agent\"` and only `--actor` provided (or vice versa); when `kind == \"actor\"` require `task_actor_env() == actor_value` and reject as **self-ack** when `run_started.actor == actor_value` and `task_actor_env() == actor_value` (recovery message: `artagents next --project <slug>` for all mismatch classes). On success, emit `step_attested` directly inside `gate_command` (no companion `record_dispatch_complete` for attested \u2014 the event itself advances the cursor). Path discipline: every event the gate writes must carry the path-qualified `plan_step_id` constructed from the current frame stack (e.g., `\"s2/c1\"`); top-level steps use their bare id. Add kernel-level helpers next to `record_dispatch_complete`: `record_step_attested(decision, attestor_kind, attestor_id, evidence)`, `record_nested_entered(decision, child_plan_hash)`, `record_nested_exited(decision, returncode)` \u2014 public for symmetry but the gate's auto-traversal uses private helpers internally; reserved for Phase 5 lifecycle verbs. Add a one-line module comment documenting that Phase 2 attested/nested are kernel-only and Phase 5 will add `artagents ack` / `artagents next` verbs that drive these helpers. Keep this module under ~400 lines.",

    "executor_notes": "gate.py refactored end-to-end per Step 4. Verified by reading the file and running the 24 Phase-1 kernel tests + the full 565-test suite (all green). Key landing points: (1) derive_cursor(plan, events) replays events.jsonl with a frame stack [_Frame(plan, path_prefix, child_index)]; nested_entered pushes (asserts NestedStep at top.child_index, pushes child plan with path_prefix=top.path_prefix+(step.id,)); nested_exited pops + advances parent; step_completed/step_attested advance top; step_dispatched/run_started are no-ops; partial-replay reconstructible from events alone. (2) Entry-loop after derive: while top frame exhausted -> auto-emit nested_exited(returncode=0) with slash-path then pop+advance; while current step is NestedStep -> compute child_hash via _compute_inline_plan_hash (sha256:<hex> of canonical_event_json(plan.to_dict())) and emit nested_entered, push frame, recompute. (3) match_attested_command: shlex.split tokenizes; single-pass while loop pulls --agent <id>, --actor <name>, --evidence <path> (repeatable, appended); rejoins remaining via ' '.join(shlex.quote(t)) and compares to expected_prefix (=step.command); returns (matched, AttestedArgs(agent, actor, evidence)). (4) Per-kind dispatch: code uses strict command==step.command equality; attested uses match_attested_command + _validate_attested_identity; nested handled by entry-loop, never reaches caller. (5) Attested rejection paths all route through _reject(abort=False) -> recovery 'artagents next --project <slug>': neither --agent/--actor; both; ack.kind=agent without --agent / ack.kind=actor without --actor (kind-mismatch); ack.kind=actor with task_actor_env() != args.actor; self-ack when run_started.actor==args.actor==task_actor_env(). FLAG-005 (agent self-ack out of scope in V1 because agents do not start runs) documented inline at the self-ack check. (6) step_attested emitted INLINE inside _dispatch_attested (lines 288-291); record_dispatch_complete checks step_kind=='attested' and returns without emitting step_completed -> attested events self-advance the cursor; FLAG-007 verified by grep showing record_step_attested has zero call sites inside artagents/core/task/gate.py (only definition + __init__.py re-export). (7) Path discipline: every gate-emitted event uses path_str = STEP_PATH_SEP.join(current_path) where current_path = top.path_prefix + (step.id,); top-level steps have path_prefix=() so path_str equals bare id (preserves Phase 1 wire shape). nested_entered/nested_exited carry the parent step's path; leaf step_dispatched/step_completed/step_attested events carry the full slash-path. (8) Public Phase-5 helpers record_step_attested/record_nested_entered/record_nested_exited live next to record_dispatch_complete with zero in-gate callers and docstrings noting 'Reserved for Phase 5 lifecycle verbs; gate emits inline in Phase 2'. (9) Module-level docstring explicitly documents Phase 2 kernel-only / Phase 5 verb wiring. (10) File length 439 lines (~10% over ~400 target; symmetry with Phase 5 helpers forced this and is acceptable). No new CLI verbs added to pipeline.py (Phase 5 boundary respected). Replace the existing `cursor = sum(1 for event in events if event.get('kind') == 'step_completed')` (~gate.py:67-69) with `derive_cursor(plan, events) -> CursorPath` that replays `events.jsonl` left-to-right with a frame stack initialized to `[Frame(plan=plan, child_index=0)]`: on `nested_entered` look up the step at the current path, push `Frame(plan=current_step.plan, child_index=0)`; on `nested_exited` pop and increment the parent frame's `child_index`; on `step_completed` or `step_attested` increment the top frame's `child_index`; `step_dispatched` does not advance. Result is a path tuple pointing at the next step or a `done` sentinel when root frame's `child_index == len(steps)`. Add an entry-loop after cursor derivation: while the step at the cursor is a `NestedStep`, compute `child_plan_hash = compute_plan_hash` over the inlined child plan's serialized form, emit `nested_entered`, push the frame, recompute the cursor; when a frame's `child_index == len(steps)`, emit `nested_exited` (returncode 0 on clean fall-through), pop, continue. Implement per-kind command matching in `gate_command`: for `code` keep `command == step.command` strict equality; for `attested` add `match_attested_command(incoming: str, expected_prefix: str) -> tuple[bool, AttestedArgs]` that tokenizes `incoming` via `shlex.split`, splits off recognized identity/evidence flags (`--agent <id>`, `--actor <name>`, `--evidence <path>` repeatable), rejoins remainder via `shlex.quote` and compares to `expected_prefix`, and returns parsed `AttestedArgs(agent: str | None, actor: str | None, evidence: tuple[str,...])`; `nested` is not caller-driven (handled by entry-loop). Implement attested gating rules: reject when neither `--agent` nor `--actor` is present, or both are present; reject when `step.ack.kind == \"agent\"` and only `--actor` provided (or vice versa); when `kind == \"actor\"` require `task_actor_env() == actor_value` and reject as **self-ack** when `run_started.actor == actor_value` and `task_actor_env() == actor_value` (recovery message: `artagents next --project <slug>` for all mismatch classes). On success, emit `step_attested` directly inside `gate_command` (no companion `record_dispatch_complete` for attested \u2014 the event itself advances the cursor). Path discipline: every event the gate writes must carry the path-qualified `plan_step_id` constructed from the current frame stack (e.g., `\"s2/c1\"`); top-level steps use their bare id. Add kernel-level helpers next to `record_dispatch_complete`: `record_step_attested(decision, attestor_kind, attestor_id, evidence)`, `record_nested_entered(decision, child_plan_hash)`, `record_nested_exited(decision, returncode)` \u2014 public for symmetry but the gate's auto-traversal uses private helpers internally; reserved for Phase 5 lifecycle verbs. Add a one-line module comment documenting that Phase 2 attested/nested are kernel-only and Phase 5 will add `artagents ack` / `artagents next` verbs that drive these helpers. Keep this module under ~400 lines.",
      "depends_on": [
        "T1",
        "T2"
      ],
      "status": "done",
      "executor_notes": "gate.py refactored end-to-end per Step 4. Verified by reading the file and running the 24 Phase-1 kernel tests + the full 565-test suite (all green). Key landing points: (1) derive_cursor(plan, events) replays events.jsonl with a frame stack [_Frame(plan, path_prefix, child_index)]; nested_entered pushes (asserts NestedStep at top.child_index, pushes child plan with path_prefix=top.path_prefix+(step.id,)); nested_exited pops + advances parent; step_completed/step_attested advance top; step_dispatched/run_started are no-ops; partial-replay reconstructible from events alone. (2) Entry-loop after derive: while top frame exhausted -> auto-emit nested_exited(returncode=0) with slash-path then pop+advance; while current step is NestedStep -> compute child_hash via _compute_inline_plan_hash (sha256:<hex> of canonical_event_json(plan.to_dict())) and emit nested_entered, push frame, recompute. (3) match_attested_command: shlex.split tokenizes; single-pass while loop pulls --agent <id>, --actor <name>, --evidence <path> (repeatable, appended); rejoins remaining via ' '.join(shlex.quote(t)) and compares to expected_prefix (=step.command); returns (matched, AttestedArgs(agent, actor, evidence)). (4) Per-kind dispatch: code uses strict command==step.command equality; attested uses match_attested_command + _validate_attested_identity; nested handled by entry-loop, never reaches caller. (5) Attested rejection paths all route through _reject(abort=False) -> recovery 'artagents next --project <slug>': neither --agent/--actor; both; ack.kind=agent without --agent / ack.kind=actor without --actor (kind-mismatch); ack.kind=actor with task_actor_env() != args.actor; self-ack when run_started.actor==args.actor==task_actor_env(). FLAG-005 (agent self-ack out of scope in V1 because agents do not start runs) documented inline at the self-ack check. (6) step_attested emitted INLINE inside _dispatch_attested (lines 288-291); record_dispatch_complete checks step_kind=='attested' and returns without emitting step_completed -> attested events self-advance the cursor; FLAG-007 verified by grep showing record_step_attested has zero call sites inside artagents/core/task/gate.py (only definition + __init__.py re-export). (7) Path discipline: every gate-emitted event uses path_str = STEP_PATH_SEP.join(current_path) where current_path = top.path_prefix + (step.id,); top-level steps have path_prefix=() so path_str equals bare id (preserves Phase 1 wire shape). nested_entered/nested_exited carry the parent step's path; leaf step_dispatched/step_completed/step_attested events carry the full slash-path. (8) Public Phase-5 helpers record_step_attested/record_nested_entered/record_nested_exited live next to record_dispatch_complete with zero in-gate callers and docstrings noting 'Reserved for Phase 5 lifecycle verbs; gate emits inline in Phase 2'. (9) Module-level docstring explicitly documents Phase 2 kernel-only / Phase 5 verb wiring. (10) File length 439 lines (~10% over ~400 target; symmetry with Phase 5 helpers forced this and is acceptable). No new CLI verbs added to pipeline.py (Phase 5 boundary respected). No structural changes to runner.py or executor/runner.py (T5's audit scope).",

      "description": "Write the new and extended kernel tests per Step 7. CREATE `tests/test_task_plan_schema.py` covering: (a) legacy `{id, command}` plan loads as a single `code`-kind step (back-compat); (b) explicit `kind: \"code\"` form normalizes such that the *normalized serialized form* (`to_dict` output) is identical to the normalized form of the legacy literal \u2014 assert this rather than asserting `compute_plan_hash` equality between raw legacy and raw canonical literals (per FLAG-008: `compute_plan_hash` hashes the on-disk canonical-event-json of the raw payload, so legacy and canonical raw literals will hash differently \u2014 what we assert is `to_dict` round-trip determinism on the canonical form); (c) `kind: \"code\"` with command `python3 -m artagents orchestrators run ...` is rejected at `load_plan` with `TaskPlanError` mentioning \"nested\"; (d) sibling-id collision in one frame is rejected; identical leaf ids across different subtrees are accepted; (e) pack-side `OrchestratorDefinition` legacy manifests still validate via `validate_orchestrator_definition` with one fixture per `RUNTIME_KINDS={python,command}`. CREATE `tests/test_task_kernel_attested.py` driving the gate directly with synthetic argv (no CLI verb yet): reject when neither `--agent` nor `--actor` present (recovery `artagents next ...`); reject when both present; `step.ack.kind=\"agent\"` succeeds with `--agent <id>`, emits `step_attested` with `attestor_kind=\"agent\"`, `attestor_id=<id>`, `evidence=()` and a path-qualified `plan_step_id`; `step.ack.kind=\"actor\"` requires `ARTAGENTS_ACTOR=<same>`, mismatch rejects; **self-ack rejected** when `run_started.actor == ARTAGENTS_ACTOR == --actor` for the same attested step (reason \"self-ack rejected\"); evidence `--evidence a.json --evidence b.json` records both, canonical command match still succeeds because the matcher strips evidence tokens; `verify_chain` returns `ok=True` after attested events. CREATE `tests/test_task_kernel_nested.py` driving the gate to walk a nested plan: mutating any child step changes parent `compute_plan_hash` (structural pinning); walking parent `[s1, s2(nested=[c1, c2]), s3]` auto-emits `nested_entered(\"s2\", child_plan_hash)`, accepts `c1` (event `step_completed` with `plan_step_id=\"s2/c1\"`), then `c2` (`\"s2/c2\"`), auto-emits `nested_exited(\"s2\", returncode=0)`, then accepts `s3`; two-level nesting `[s1, s2(nested=[c1, c2(nested=[g1])])]` produces path sequence `s1, s2/c1, s2/c2/g1` with matching enter/exit events; sibling subtrees with reused leaf ids `[s1(nested=[c1]), s2(nested=[c1])]` disambiguate via `s1/c1` vs `s2/c1`; `derive_cursor` is correct on partial replay (resume mid-nested-walk). EXTEND `tests/test_task_kernel_gate.py` with: regression that existing Phase 1 helper (`_write_plan` style with bare `{id, command}`) still gates correctly, and that Phase 1 events for top-level code steps keep their bare `plan_step_id` payload shape.",

\"--evidence run-1.json --evidence approval.json\",\n            [],\n            root=tmp,\n        )\n        assert d2.plan_step_id == \"s2\", d2.plan_step_id\n        assert d2.step_kind == \"attested\"\n\n        # Step 3: nested step auto-traversed; the gate emits nested_entered(\"s3\", child_hash)\n        # then dispatches the child step at path \"s3/c1\".\n        d3 = gate_command(slug, \"echo child\", [], root=tmp)\n        assert d3.plan_step_id == \"s3/c1\", d3.plan_step_id\n        assert d3.step_kind == \"code\"\n        record_dispatch_complete(d3, 0)\n\n        # Trigger lazy nested_exited emission via a final gate call (plan exhausted).\n        try:\n            gate_command(slug, \"anything\", [], root=tmp)\n        except Exception as exc:\n            assert \"abort\" in str(getattr(exc, \"recovery\", \"\")), repr(exc)\n\n        events = read_events(events_path)\n        kinds = [(e[\"kind\"], e.get(\"plan_step_id\")) for e in events]\n        expected = [\n            (\"run_started\", None),\n            (\"step_dispatched\", \"s1\"),\n            (\"step_completed\", \"s1\"),\n            (\"step_attested\", \"s2\"),\n            (\"nested_entered\", \"s3\"),\n            (\"step_dispatched\", \"s3/c1\"),\n            (\"step_completed\", \"s3/c1\"),\n            (\"nested_exited\", \"s3\"),\n        ]\n        assert kinds == expected, f\"event sequence mismatch: {kinds}\"\n\n        attested = next(e for e in events if e[\"kind\"] == \"step_attested\")\n        assert attested[\"attestor_kind\"] == \"agent\"\n        assert attested[\"attestor_id\"] == \"claude\"\n        assert attested[\"evidence\"] == [\"run-1.json\", \"approval.json\"]\n        assert attested[\"plan_step_id\"] == \"s2\"\n\n        nested_in = next(e for e in events if e[\"kind\"] == \"nested_entered\")\n        assert nested_in[\"plan_step_id\"] == \"s3\"\n        assert nested_in[\"child_plan_hash\"].startswith(\"sha256:\")\n        assert len(nested_in[\"child_plan_hash\"]) == len(\"sha256:\") + 64\n\n        ok, last_index, error = verify_chain(events_path)\n        assert ok, f\"verify_chain failed: error={error}\"\n        assert last_index == len(events) - 1\n\n        print(f\"OK: {len(events)} events, verify_chain={ok}, attested.evidence={attested['evidence']}\")\n        return 0\n    finally:\n        shutil.rmtree(tmp, ignore_errors=True)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    sys.exit(main())\n"
}
